# Idioms & Phrases

Idioms asked and the meaning options offered.

- Distinct entries: **11,530**
- Questions processed: **2,678**

`asMain` = the entry was asked about; `asOption` = it appeared among another question's options; `asOptionCorrect` = it was the answer in that question. A question never counts its own main term as an option (its options are that question's meaning options), and duplicates inside one option set count once. Ranked by `importance = 3*asMain + asOption`. Entries are grouped case- and diacritic-insensitively (and, for idioms, ignoring a leading `to `), so `Abandon`/`abandon` and `To spill the beans`/`spill the beans` are one row; the first spelling seen is shown.
